# Linux v1 NVIDIA runtime lock (release issue P02).
#
# What this file is: exact, hash-pinned versions for every third-party
# distribution in the Linux NVIDIA release profile, for Linux x86_64 on
# CPython 3.13. It is usable both as `pip install -r` input (the
# verified consumption below) and as `pip install -c` constraints
# alongside index-published or wheel-file requirements.
#
# Profile roots (NOT pinned here; installed from the local tree):
#   ./[nvidia]             root GUI distribution + torch for GUI startup
#   ./services[desktop]    complete Qt-free services aggregate
#   ./native               launcher only (no third-party runtime deps)
# The resolver emits these roots as bare local paths; bare paths are
# illegal in a constraints file and unverifiable under pip's
# hash-checking mode (a directory has no digest), so they are stripped
# during generation (see below) and installed explicitly instead.
#
# Indexes: PyPI plus the PyTorch cu129 wheel index. Each entry's
# `# from ...` annotation records which index supplied it; the cu129
# entries are the torch family, its CUDA wheels, and version-constrained
# torch dependencies (fsspec/mpmath/networkx/sympy) whose locked files
# are byte-identical to PyPI's. CPU wheels are not a release target, so
# no CPU index appears here.
#
# Verified consumption (fresh isolated virtualenv, CPython 3.13):
#   pip install -r package/constraints-linux-nvidia-cu129.txt
#   pip install --no-deps ./[nvidia] ./services[desktop] ./native
#   pip check
# Step 1 installs only index artifacts, every one hash-verified. Step 2
# builds the three profile roots from this tree (their declared build
# backends fetch setuptools>=80.9.0,<82 plus wheel into ephemeral
# isolated build environments per pyproject.toml; that build-system pin,
# guarded by test_dependency_constraints.py for issue #2057, is
# independent of the runtime setuptools pin locked below). `pip check`
# then validates the union, which is the mutual-compatibility proof.
#
# Verification evidence (2026-10-08; fresh `uv venv --seed` virtualenv,
# CPython 3.13.5 with pip 26.2.1, installs exactly as documented above):
#   step 1 installed all 352 pins with hash verification (exit 0);
#   step 2 installed airunner/airunner-services/airunner-native 6.1.3
#   from this tree (exit 0); `pip freeze` matches every locked version
#   (modulo pip's own -/._ name normalization); `pip check` verdict:
#   "No broken requirements found." (exit 0). Installed torch
#   2.13.0+cu129 metadata requires setuptools>=77.0.3 with no upper
#   bound, so the locked runtime setuptools==84.0.0 satisfies it; the
#   <82 build-system pin (issue #2057) governs only the ephemeral
#   isolated build environments, not this runtime set.
#
# Regeneration (from the repository root; needs network and uv):
#   uv pip compile --python <a CPython 3.13 interpreter> \
#     --generate-hashes --emit-index-url --emit-index-annotation \
#     --index-strategy unsafe-best-match --no-header \
#     -o /tmp/p02-lock-raw.txt /dev/stdin <<'EOF'
#   --extra-index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu129
#   ./[nvidia]
#   ./services[desktop]
#   ./native
#   EOF
# Then delete the three bare local-path root lines (and their `# via`
# comment tails) and prepend this header. `--index-strategy
# unsafe-best-match` matches pip's own multi-index semantics (highest
# satisfactory version across the union of indexes); uv's default
# first-index strategy cannot resolve this profile because the cu129
# index shadows several PyPI packages at older versions.
#
# Generated 2026-10-08 with uv 0.12.3 for CPython 3.13.5 on Linux
# x86_64, from worktree commit 742a686c2 plus the uncommitted P02
# profile changes (root `nvidia` extra, declared starlette/shiboken6).
--index-url https://pypi.org/simple
--extra-index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu129
